{"type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01C9i4V7aRnufsdrNkjzh7Fm","type":"tool_result","content":[{"type":"text","text":"I now have comprehensive research findings. Here is the full report.\n\n---\n\n## Research Report: Intelligent Medical Software (IMS) by Meditab Software / MedPharm Services LLC\n\n### 1. Company Overview & History\n\n**Founding:** The Patel family founded Meditab Software, Inc. in **1998** in Sacramento, CA. The origin story is that the family was frustrated with the inadequate EHR software in their family-owned pharmacy and decided to build their own. Mike Patel (CEO) earned his Pharmacy degree from Drake University at age 20. Kalpesh Patel co-founded the company and later departed to form MedPharm Services LLC.\n\n**Headquarters:** 8795 Folsom Blvd Suite 205, Sacramento, California 95826.\n\n**Size:** Approximately **606 employees** across 5 continents (US, India, Philippines, and other offices). One review-aggregator source cited 32 employees and $3.8M revenue, but this appears outdated or inaccurate given other data. The company has offices in 10 countries including the US, Canada, UK, Australia, and India.\n\n**Customer Base:** Meditab claims to have helped over **14,000 providers** across the country. Earlier references mention 2,200+ clients. The company claims a **99.8% customer retention rate**, 94% client satisfaction, and 95% client recommendation rate.\n\n**Funding:** Unfunded / bootstrapped. No venture capital or private equity backing found.\n\n### 2. Relationship Between MedPharm Services LLC and Meditab Software\n\nMedPharm Services LLC was founded by **Kalpesh Patel**, who co-founded Meditab Software. After leaving day-to-day operations at Meditab (where he helped build the company to 350+ employees), Patel purchased and operated pharmacies in California and then created MedPharm Services to provide implementation, support services, remote staffing, EHR consulting, and management/consulting services to healthcare companies. MedPharm Services is listed on the ONC CHPL as a developer associated with IMS, specifically described as \"an affiliate of\" Meditab Software.\n\n### 3. Product Capabilities & Modules\n\nIMS is marketed as an **all-in-one practice automation system** for the ambulatory medical office. Key modules and capabilities confirmed across multiple sources:\n\n| Module | Details |\n|--------|---------|\n| **EHR/EMR** | Customizable electronic health records with specialty templates for 40+ specialties |\n| **Practice Management** | Scheduling, appointment management, task management, performance management |\n| **Medical Billing / RCM** | Claims submission, payer rules engine, financial reporting, ICD-10 support |\n| **E-Prescribing** | Integrated with pharmacies; includes IMS Hub-Rx for prescription flow management |\n| **Patient Portal** | \"IMS Care\" -- patient portal and mobile app for records access, appointment management, secure messaging |\n| **Telehealth** | \"Televisit\" -- HIPAA-compliant telemedicine integrated directly within IMS |\n| **Lab Integration** | Integrated laboratory and diagnostic ordering, lab result tracking |\n| **Document Management** | Document management with faxing capabilities |\n| **Reporting/Analytics** | Practice analytics, KPI dashboards, population health management |\n| **Clinical Decision Support** | Built-in clinical decision support tools |\n| **Payment Processing** | IMS Payment Processing, point-of-sale system |\n| **IVR & Reminders** | Automated prescription refills, patient reminders, alerts |\n| **Imaging** | Document management imaging (through reseller MICA) |\n| **Insurance Auth** | Prior authorization templates |\n| **Chat** | IMS Chat for internal communication |\n\nAdditionally, Meditab offers a **separate pharmacy product** called **IPS (Intelligent Pharmacy Software)**, launched in 2003, for retail, long-term care, 340B, specialty, and compounding pharmacies.\n\n### 4. Deployment Model\n\nIMS supports **multiple deployment options**:\n- **Cloud / SaaS / Web-based**\n- **On-premise server**\n- **Hybrid**\n- Compatible with **Windows, Mac, Linux, iOS, Android**\n- Mobile app: **IMSGo**\n- Average implementation time: **22 days**\n\n### 5. Specialties Served\n\nConfirmed specialties from Black Book awards and product pages include:\n- Allergy & Immunology\n- Cardiology\n- Chiropractic\n- Community Health / FQHCs\n- Cosmetic\n- Dermatology\n- Endocrinology\n- Fertility\n- Gastroenterology\n- General Practice / Internal Medicine\n- Nephrology\n- Neurology\n- OB/GYN\n- Oncology\n- Orthopedics\n- Otolaryngology (ENT)\n- Pain Management\n- Pediatrics\n- Plastic Surgery\n- Podiatry\n- Psychiatry\n- Radiology\n- Rheumatology\n- Urology\n\nThe product claims 40+ specialty modules total.\n\n### 6. Third-Party Review Findings\n\n**G2:**\n- Ease of use score: **8.1/10**\n- Quality of support score: **7.2/10**\n- Overall: **4 stars**\n- Earned \"High Performers\" position on G2 Grid Report for EHR, Spring 2022\n- Reviews range widely from 2/5 to 5/5\n\n**Capterra:**\n- Reviews range from **4/5 to 5/5**\n- The product page exists at `capterra.com/p/242487/Intelligent-Medical-Software/reviews/`\n\n**Software Advice (Gartner):**\n- Active profile with reviews dating back to at least 2023\n\n**EHR Reviews (ehrreviews.com):**\n- Overall: **4.5/5** (editorial rating)\n- Ease of use: 4.0/5\n- Customer support: 3.8/5\n- Value for money: 4.1/5\n- Functionality: 3.9/5\n\n**HealthAIDB:**\n- Charting/Document Management: 9.5/10\n- Templates: 9.4/10\n- HIPAA Compliance: 8.5/10\n\n**Enlyft (market intelligence):**\n- Market share: **0.01%** in healthcare category\n- 20 companies tracked in their database\n- 54% of users are small practices (<50 employees)\n- 92% of customers are in the United States\n- Top industries: Medical Practice (21%), Hospital & Health Care (16%)\n\n### 7. What Users Say (Concrete Quotes)\n\n**Positive:**\n- \"IMS in general is very user friendly. It is designed very nicely where we can access patient information basically from any tabs.\"\n- \"One of the best features of this software is the ability to customize to the needs of the practice and the individual.\"\n- \"IMS improved the AR -- in one provider increasing revenue by 30% in the first 8 weeks.\"\n- \"Meditab has the best customer service. The agents are always ready to help in a very professional and respectful manner.\"\n- \"We have not had a single clinic that didn't say this is the best EHR we've ever had.\"\n- \"IMS is one system, one log-in, and that's very convenient for us as a federally qualified health center that provides multiple service lines -- everything from podiatry to medical, dental, optometry, behavioral health, and obstetrics.\" (FQHC user)\n- \"A biller's dream\" with customization for multiple providers and fee schedules.\n\n**Negative:**\n- \"It is the least efficient software I have ever used, 5 clicks where 1-2 would do.\"\n- \"We were told the program could integrate various aspects of the office but were not told we would have to buy 3rd party software and have new programming to achieve this.\"\n- \"The new Rx feature is complicated and the software is expensive.\"\n- \"When placing medical terminology for diagnoses, there are too many dropdown choices and some are not what the team is looking for.\"\n- Customer service response times inconsistent -- some reports of up to two days for replies.\n\n### 8. Industry Recognition\n\n**Black Book Research Awards:**\n- **2023:** 13 awards (record for the company)\n- **2024:** 13 awards again, including #1 in Endocrinology (up from #12 the prior year), plus Allergy & Immunology, Chiropractic, Dermatology, Gastroenterology, Internal Medicine, Nephrology, ENT, Pediatrics, Plastic Surgery, Podiatry, Urology, and Best Ambulatory EHR for Small Practices (2-5 physicians)\n- **2025:** 9 individual category wins\n\n**KLAS:** Recognized by KLAS as a \"top-rated vendor\" though no specific KLAS score or detailed report was found publicly.\n\n**ONC Certification:** Certified via Drummond Group for ONC Health IT Certification (2015 Edition Cures Update), for IMS V14 SP1.\n\n### 9. Acquisitions & Partnerships\n\n- **Fox Meadows Software** (September 2024): Jointly acquired by Meditab and DrCatalyst. Fox Meadows, founded in 1994, provided software and RCM solutions to medical practices.\n- **Brevium integration** (April 2024): Patient reactivation and retention platform integrated into IMS, specifically for allergy/immunotherapy patient workflows.\n- **DrCatalyst**: Described as a \"leading partner\" of Meditab providing virtual medical assistant and remote staffing solutions with 1,200+ remote staff. Relationship described as \"affiliated\" rather than a clear parent-subsidiary structure.\n- **MICA Information Systems**: #1 Value-Added Reseller (VAR) for IMS in the US, founded in 1979. MICA provides implementation, support, and billing services on top of IMS.\n- **Suite Rx**: Selected as a VAR for IPS (pharmacy software) as early as 2007.\n\n### 10. Pricing\n\n- Starting at approximately **$150-$199/month per user** (subscription model)\n- For larger practices (~100 users): approximately **$10,000-$15,000/month**\n- Free trial available\n- Custom quotes recommended\n- Implementation costs are not publicly disclosed\n\n### 11. Interoperability & Data Standards\n\nSupported standards per product documentation:\n- **FHIR R4** (including Bulk Data export)\n- **HL7 v2**\n- **HL7 C-CDA XML**\n- **DICOM**\n- **SNOMED CT, ICD-10, LOINC**\n- EHI Export capability for individual patients and broader populations\n- Public API documentation available at `meditab.com/api`\n\n### 12. KLAS / Definitive Healthcare\n\n- Meditab is **not** a major player on Definitive Healthcare's hospital EHR market share lists (those are dominated by Epic, Oracle/Cerner, MEDITECH). Meditab is an ambulatory-focused EHR, not a hospital/inpatient system.\n- KLAS recognition is referenced on Meditab's marketing materials but no detailed KLAS report with scores was found publicly.\n\n### 13. Summary Assessment\n\nMeditab IMS is a **mid-market, ambulatory-focused, all-in-one EHR/PM/billing platform** with strong specialty customization (40+ specialties), particularly dominant in smaller niche specialties (allergy, endocrinology, dermatology, podiatry, etc.) where it consistently wins Black Book awards. The company is family-founded, bootstrapped, and based in Sacramento with roughly 600 employees globally. It serves primarily small-to-medium practices (2-5 physicians is a sweet spot) and FQHCs. The product offers both cloud and on-premise deployment. User reviews are polarized -- many praise the customizability and all-in-one nature, while others criticize workflow inefficiency (too many clicks) and cost. Market share is very small (0.01% per Enlyft) compared to major EHR vendors.\n\nSources:\n- [Capterra - IMS Reviews](https://www.capterra.com/p/242487/Intelligent-Medical-Software/reviews/)\n- [Software Advice - Industry Recognition\n\n**Black Book Research Awards:**\n- **2023:** 13 awards (record for the company)\n- **2024:** 13 awards again, including #1 in Endocrinology (up from #12 the prior year), plus Allergy & Immunology, Chiropractic, Dermatology, Gastroenterology, Internal Medicine, Nephrology, ENT, Pediatrics, Plastic Surgery, Podiatry, Urology, and Best Ambulatory EHR for Small Practices (2-5 physicians)\n- **2025:** 9 individual category wins\n\n**KLAS:** Recognized by KLAS as a \"top-rated vendor\" though no specific KLAS score or detailed report was found publicly.\n\n**ONC Certification:** Certified via Drummond Group for ONC Health IT Certification (2015 Edition Cures Update), for IMS V14 SP1.\n\n### 9. Acquisitions & Partnerships\n\n- **Fox Meadows Software** (September 2024): Jointly acquired by Meditab and DrCatalyst. Fox Meadows, founded in 1994, provided software and RCM solutions to medical practices.\n- **Brevium integration** (April 2024): Patient reactivation and retention platform integrated into IMS, specifically for allergy/immunotherapy patient workflows.\n- **DrCatalyst**: Described as a \"leading partner\" of Meditab providing virtual medical assistant and remote staffing solutions with 1,200+ remote staff. Relationship described as \"affiliated\" rather than a clear parent-subsidiary structure.\n- **MICA Information Systems**: #1 Value-Added Reseller (VAR) for IMS in the US, founded in 1979. MICA provides implementation, support, and billing services on top of IMS.\n- **Suite Rx**: Selected as a VAR for IPS (pharmacy software) as early as 2007.\n\n### 10. Pricing\n\n- Starting at approximately **$150-$199/month per user** (subscription model)\n- For larger practices (~100 users): approximately **$10,000-$15,000/month**\n- Free trial available\n- Custom quotes recommended\n- Implementation costs are not publicly disclosed\n\n### 11. Interoperability & Data Standards\n\nSupported standards per product documentation:\n- **FHIR R4** (including Bulk Data export)\n- **HL7 v2**\n- **HL7 C-CDA XML**\n- **DICOM**\n- **SNOMED CT, ICD-10, LOINC**\n- EHI Export capability for individual patients and broader populations\n- Public API documentation available at `meditab.com/api`\n\n### 12. KLAS / Definitive Healthcare\n\n- Meditab is **not** a major player on Definitive Healthcare's hospital EHR market share lists (those are dominated by Epic, Oracle/Cerner, MEDITECH). Meditab is an ambulatory-focused EHR, not a hospital/inpatient system.\n- KLAS recognition is referenced on Meditab's marketing materials but no detailed KLAS report with scores was found publicly.\n\n### 13. Summary Assessment\n\nMeditab IMS is a **mid-market, ambulatory-focused, all-in-one EHR/PM/billing platform** with strong specialty customization (40+ specialties), particularly dominant in smaller niche specialties (allergy, endocrinology, dermatology, podiatry, etc.) where it consistently wins Black Book awards. The company is family-founded, bootstrapped, and based in Sacramento with roughly 600 employees globally. It serves primarily small-to-medium practices (2-5 physicians is a sweet spot) and FQHCs. The product offers both cloud and on-premise deployment. User reviews are polarized -- many praise the customizability and all-in-one nature, while others criticize workflow inefficiency (too many clicks) and cost. Market share is very small (0.01% per Enlyft) compared to major EHR vendors.\n\nSources:\n- [Capterra - IMS Reviews](https://www.capterra.com/p/242487/Intelligent-Medical-Software/reviews/)\n- [Software Advice - {"type":"assistant","message":{"model":"claude-opus-4-6","id":"msg_01MU8Hx124rYuUz6JK1qrakY","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"tool_use","id":"toolu_018wKGrkWeeHWDyXpgcAUiCJ","name":"Write","input":{"file_path":"/home/jmandel/hobby/ehi-export-analysis/results/medpharm-services-llc--intelligent-medical-software-ims/product-research.md","content":"# MedPharm Services LLC / Meditab — Product Research\n\nResearched: 2026-02-15\nDeveloper website: https://www.meditab.com\n\n## Overview\n\nMedPharm Services LLC is an affiliate of Meditab Software, Inc., a family-founded EHR company established in 1998 in Sacramento, CA by the Patel family. The origin story involves the family's frustration with inadequate software at their own pharmacy. MedPharm Services LLC was created by co-founder Kalpesh Patel and is the entity listed on ONC CHPL as the developer of IMS. Meditab itself has approximately 600 employees across offices in 10 countries (US, India, Philippines, UK, Australia, Canada, and others).\n\nMeditab is a small-to-mid-tier ambulatory EHR vendor. They claim 41,000 healthcare professionals and 1,600+ clinics using IMS. Per Enlyft, their market share is approximately 0.01% in healthcare IT. They are bootstrapped (no VC or PE funding). The company is primarily known for deep specialty customization across 40+ medical specialties, and consistently wins Black Book Research awards in niche specialty categories. They also make a separate pharmacy software product called IPS (Intelligent Pharmacy Software).\n\nIn September 2024, Meditab and affiliated company DrCatalyst jointly acquired Fox Meadows Software, a practice software and RCM vendor founded in 1994. MICA Information Systems (founded 1979) is their top Value-Added Reseller in the US.\n\n## Product: Intelligent Medical Software (IMS)\n\nCHPL ID: 9739\n\n### What It Is\n\nIMS is a **Complete EHR** (classified as such on ONC certification) for ambulatory/outpatient settings. It is an all-in-one practice automation system encompassing EHR, practice management, medical billing/RCM, e-prescribing, patient portal, telemedicine, and reporting — all within a single platform and single database. The certified product is the whole IMS platform, not a component of something larger.\n\nThe product is certified for 50 ONC criteria spanning clinical data (a), care coordination (b), clinical quality measures (c), security (d), patient access (e), public health reporting (f), API/FHIR (g), and direct messaging (h). This is a broad certification indicating the product handles the full range of ambulatory clinical, billing, and interoperability functions.\n\nIMS supports both cloud/SaaS and on-premise deployment. It runs on Windows, Mac, and Linux. The underlying database is Sybase SQL Anywhere. Average implementation time is reported as 22 days.\n\n### Users & Market\n\n**Target users:** Physicians, nurses, billing staff, practice managers, and patients (via portal). The SED intended user description lists \"General Medicine, Allergy, Pediatrics, Fertility, Internal Medicine.\"\n\n**Settings:** Primarily small-to-medium ambulatory practices (2-5 physicians is described as a sweet spot) and FQHCs. Per Enlyft, 54% of users are in practices with fewer than 50 employees, and 92% are in the United States. Not a hospital/inpatient system.\n\n**Specialties:** 40+ specialties with dedicated specialty-specific modules and templates. The origin story involves the family's frustration with inadequate software at their own pharmacy. MedPharm Services LLC was created by co-founder Kalpesh Patel and is the entity listed on ONC CHPL as the developer of IMS. Meditab itself has approximately 600 employees across offices in 10 countries (US, India, Philippines, UK, Australia, Canada, and others).\n\nMeditab is a small-to-mid-tier ambulatory EHR vendor. They claim 41,000 healthcare professionals and 1,600+ clinics using IMS. Per Enlyft, their market share is approximately 0.01% in healthcare IT. They are bootstrapped (no VC or PE funding). The company is primarily known for deep specialty customization across 40+ medical specialties, and consistently wins Black Book Research awards in niche specialty categories. They also make a separate pharmacy software product called IPS (Intelligent Pharmacy Software).\n\nIn September 2024, Meditab and affiliated company DrCatalyst jointly acquired Fox Meadows Software, a practice software and RCM vendor founded in 1994. MICA Information Systems (founded 1979) is their top Value-Added Reseller in the US.\n\n## Product: Intelligent Medical Software (IMS)\n\nCHPL ID: 9739\n\n### What It Is\n\nIMS is a **Complete EHR** (classified as such on ONC certification) for ambulatory/outpatient settings. It is an all-in-one practice automation system encompassing EHR, practice management, medical billing/RCM, e-prescribing, patient portal, telemedicine, and reporting — all within a single platform and single database. The certified product is the whole IMS platform, not a component of something larger.\n\nThe product is certified for 50 ONC criteria spanning clinical data (a), care coordination (b), clinical quality measures (c), security (d), patient access (e), public health reporting (f), API/FHIR (g), and direct messaging (h). This is a broad certification indicating the product handles the full range of ambulatory clinical, billing, and interoperability functions.\n\nIMS supports both cloud/SaaS and on-premise deployment. It runs on Windows, Mac, and Linux. The underlying database is Sybase SQL Anywhere. Average implementation time is reported as 22 days.\n\n### Users & Market\n\n**Target users:** Physicians, nurses, billing staff, practice managers, and patients (via portal).
